## Native-first AI-authored selection contract — implemented 2026-08-14

The AI client is the creative and editorial authority. It chooses the story, composition, design World, native object
family, exact gallery IDs, and the treatment that is coherent and beautiful for the audience and content. Editable
native PowerPoint is the default whenever it can express that choice. A custom or shape-built construction is an
exception and must carry a specific AI-authored reason explaining why the native feature is insufficient; “custom is
better” is not an acceptable reason.

The engine is the catalog executor, geometry/package mechanic, and verifier—not the designer. Release-candidate specs
use `betterpptx.ai-selection-manifest.v1` with `enforcement:"required"`. Each entry binds a stable slide/object target to
the AI's chosen native selector IDs (or an explicit custom exception), plus the AI's coherence rationale. Compilation
fails on conflicts or missing forwarding instead of overwriting the choice. Required manifests also refuse freemium
substitution: the unchanged manifest must be recommitted in an entitled context.

`deck.save` now reopens the produced package and verifies the selected native state. The proof covers native SmartArt
layout/Quick Style/color IDs, chart style/color/layout states, native table style GUIDs, WordArt/Shape styles and
effects, gradients and 3-D/effect structures, Picture Styles, Model 3D views, connectors, equations, slide layouts, and
transitions. If a required choice disappears, is substituted, or cannot be proved in the package, the artifact is
deleted and delivery fails closed. Legacy specs remain advisory; this does not let the engine invent a design choice.

The semantic diagram default is therefore native SmartArt. Shape-built diagrams remain supported only as the explicit
AI-authored exception above. This supersedes historical entries below that describe world-styled shape diagrams as the
default.

## MVP scope decision — catalog closure is the cutoff (2026-08-13)

The complete catalog is the product boundary for the first launch. At the moment catalog closure is declared, **every
product capability already implemented is part of MVP**, regardless of whether its proof, approval, deployment,
provenance, package, visual, accessibility, or quality gate is still open. An unresolved gate blocks MVP launch; it
does not defer or remove that capability from MVP. Only capabilities first implemented after catalog closure, plus
clearly maintainer-only diagnostics, fixtures, UI harnesses, and research artifacts, remain continuous post-MVP work.

assembled but still awaits its applicable owner and deployment release gates. The canonical integration refresh is

Native Record Studio ownership correction (2026-08-14): a talking head is never an ordinary media object with an
ellipse, narration marker, or authored timing added afterward. The product target is an **AI-authored native
PowerPoint Record Studio capture**: narration text drives voice, synchronized presenter video, slide timing, and
optional native ink/laser actions; the engine serializes the saved Office-compatible session. Notes use one final
ownership splitter: new output writes `[BetterPPTX-narration]`, legacy `[narration]` remains readable, all text before
the marker is opaque user-owned notes, and all text after it is the editable narration source. On re-upload,
BetterPPTX may regenerate only the latter and must preserve the former. The splitter is recognized only as the final
notes block, so a marker-like phrase inside the user's free notes cannot steal ownership. The previously repaired
ordinary-media candidates remain withdrawn. The three earlier one-slide candidates are superseded. The evolved
six-slide notes-only candidate was regenerated through the public semantic MCP and is now owner-approved against
SHA-256 `dbbecd52e780f0b3cdbb9cd80d93c21a719758df96c96eecd7305f2b27c510d0`.

integration with native slide content. It does not replace the convergence mission. Any shared core, `dist`,
package, serializer, or deployment change from this track must pass the common convergence and release-safety
gates before promotion.

Owner doctrine (binding): calculation over guessing · text is the master clock · meaning-heuristics are
PROPOSALS the AI confirms · the deck communicates without the presenter and supports a designed reduced/static
mode · size beats fidelity when the eye can't tell ("presentation, not a movie") · measure the medium, never
model it · encoding/compression is never a privacy boundary · never delete research-demos artifacts · work ONLY
on the private repos (origin pptxgenjs is PUBLIC — never push proprietary there) · public clients see standard
presentation capabilities and product-generic opaque handles only · production promotion requires an immutable
reviewed private commit · Modal spend ≤ $30/mo/profile.

## 0. How to use this document

1. §1 tells you what "done" means. §2–§6 are the knowledge base (map, physics, contracts, recipes, traps).
2. §7 is the work: product layers A–F plus the cross-cutting Release Safety Plane G.
3. §8 is the phase order. **P0 is binding.** A private canary is not public launch; do not advance while any exit
   gate for the current or prior phase is red.
4. Every work item declares applicable gate classes. Numeric COM evidence is mandatory for PowerPoint-behavior
   claims, but package, deterministic, perceptual, privacy, operations, and licensing evidence are equally real.
5. Every evidence record includes: `status`, `dependencies`, `command`, `threshold`, `artifact/hash`, private
   `commit`, exact `ppBuild` where applicable, `measuredAt`, and result. Public surfaces never expose those
   maintainer internals.

### 0.1 Gate taxonomy

| Class | Proves | Minimum evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **PACKAGE** | Package/runtime integrity | repair-free PowerPoint open; parseable XML/rels; no dangling targets or invalid content types; unexplained topology recorded |
| **DETERMINISTIC** | Compiler correctness | unit/property/seeded-corpus result; exact input, output, and commit |
| **METROLOGY** | PowerPoint behavior | COM/live-render number with threshold, sample count, exact PowerPoint build, and artifact |
| **PERCEPTUAL** | Visible quality | recorded blind A/B or rubric-based human decision with contact sheet/filmstrip |
| **PRIVACY** | Product black-box behavior | adversarial schemas/results/help/health/download/package scan, including decoded BetterPPTX-owned sidecars |
| **OPERATIONS** | Deployable product behavior | cold client, restart/cross-instance durability, entitlements, metering, latency, cost, canary, and rollback |
| **LICENSING** | Distribution authority | hash-keyed source/license/version/attribution/distribution-mode record plus owner/legal approval where required |

### 0.3 Strategic call

M4 proves that the core interaction idea is viable; it does **not** prove a product. Workspace priority remains G0:
strengthen the comparator and drive the frozen reference to true three-oracle parity. Inside this track, the next
leverage is not more characters, clips, or cinematic polish. It is: (1) freeze reproducible/private inputs and
close release leaks, (2) implement the compiler order + Timeline IR contract, (3) automate exact-profile oracle/
package regression, and (4) make generated assets durable and tenant-safe. Only then multiply the roster and
direction language. Treat the first deployment as a private immutable canary; public promotion is deliberately P5.

## 1. The north star

**A repair-free, editable presentation authored as MEANING.** The AI writes story, narration, content,
`{actor, verb, target}` intent, and optional constraints — not coordinates or animation plumbing. For a declared
execution profile, the compiler jointly solves slide layout, stage, geometry, timing, and native PowerPoint
animation for the actual slide dimensions. It emits a designed reduced/static fallback for unsupported profiles
and returns one actionable storyboard containing every applied decision, measurement, degradation, refusal, and
fix. "Deterministic" means deterministic for the declared profile and versioned manifests; it is not a claim that
all PowerPoint builds/platforms share the same playback physics.

Four tests of excellence:
1. **Correctness** — repair-free package, deterministic plan, no unexplained package/convergence regression, and
   measured error budgets. The reported 0.102 in projection, 0.013–0.066 in contact, 0.0°/30 ms push, and 0.17 in
   carry endpoint are local research claims until A1/A3 reproduce them on coherent exact-build/hash evidence.
2. **Expressiveness** — the vocabulary covers useful human-direction requests (arcs, scenes, interactions,
   sound, camera), not merely what the current engine happens to support.
3. **Legibility** — the AI receives one canonical storyboard; every refusal contains `{code, severity, reason,
   fix, evidence}` and every fallback is explicit.
4. **Portability and economy** — behavior is declared per supported execution profile; the deck remains useful
   without live 3D; file-size, generation/open/render latency, memory, and cloud-cost budgets are enforced.

## 2. SYSTEM MAP (what lives where — read this instead of grepping)

Repo root `D:/PptxGenJS_Claude` is a **mixed-remotes working tree**: `origin` is public, while the active product
branch tracks the private `betterpptx-mcp` remote. `betterpptx-mcp/` is the product engine; `dist/` is a committed
build of the pptxgenjs core, while the current MCP container build defaults to rebuilding it — see §6 trap T15.
Several files below exist only in the current working tree. A path on this machine is evidence of local R&D, not
evidence that a git-based build, a second instance, or a public client can use it.

### 2.1 Synergy / presenter modules (all ESM, `betterpptx-mcp/ir/`)

| File | Role | Selected APIs and current behavior (verify source before coding) |
|---|---|---|
| `synergy-geometry.mjs` | S0 geometry kernel | `REST_FIT_VIEWPORT_PER_INCH=1454545` · `DEFAULT_CALIBRATION={kS:1,kX:-0.097,kY:0.069,kPz:0,mirrorX:false}` · `meshRestBBox(glbBuf)→{lo,hi,w,h,d,center}` · `fkJointTrajectories(glbBuf,animId,hz=30,joints)→{animId,name,durationMs,times,joints:{bone:[[x,y,z]…]}}` (bit-identical to the Python FK oracle, ~105 ms) · `skinnedPointsAt(glbBuf,animId,tSec,[{bone,rest:[x,y,z]}])→[{bone,world}]` (exact G·IBM·v — off-joint points orbit the bone) · `modelToSlide(p,ctx)→{x,y,inchesPerUnit}` with `ctx={frameBbox,viewportMargin,faceRotationY,rest,calib?}` (the geometry kernel does not apply yaw; interaction solving is calibrated only for the 0° front-facing roster basis; the presenter/camera's 180° convention is a separate visual default) · `clipEnvelope(glbBuf,animId)→{margin,lateralFrac,…}` · `buildObjectRegistry(slideSpec)` |
| `synergy-solver.mjs` | S1 interaction solver | `VERBS={touch,point-at,press}` (effector `mixamorig:RightHand`, standoffIn 0/1.0/0) · `candidateFrames(glbBuf,animId,effector,dirSign,hz)` → FK plateau candidates · `solvePlacement(req)` / `solveBest(req,{clips,heights=[6.5,5.6,5.0,4.2,3.5],margins=[1,1.4,1.8,2.0],tolIn=0.15,maxContactMs})` — FK path · **`solveBestEmpirical(req,manifest,opts)`** — empirical path used by M4 (rendered-truth timelines; §4.2) · verdicts: `FRAME_CLAMPED`, `UNREACHABLE`, `OCCLUDES_CONTENT` (a residual >0.25 in only ADDS the `UNREACHABLE` verdict — the best-scoring placement is still returned, nothing is rejected; `tolIn` is not yet enforced — T29) |
| `synergy-effects.mjs` | S2 effect synthesizer | `impulseCandidates(timeline,{minSpeed=3,maxContactMs})` · `carrySegments(timeline,{holdMs=300,minTravelIn=0.8,stepMs=100})` → grab-plateau→travel→drop-plateau, uniform-TIME resampled · `pathDataFromSamples(samples)` → `"M 0 0 L x y … E"` slide-fraction relative path · `synthesizeEffect(verb,contact)` → animation array for push / knock-over / carry |
| `synergy-compiler.mjs` | S3 authoring surface | `expandInteractions(spec,{castAssets})` — lowers `slides[].interactions` to solved presenter instances + contact-timed effects; records `spec._synergyPlan` · `planInteractions(spec,opts)` — dry-run. Exact skip codes: `INTERACTION_NO_ACTOR`, `INTERACTION_NO_TIMELINES`, `INTERACTION_NO_TARGET`, `INTERACTION_TARGET_AUTHORED`, `INTERACTION_UNSOLVED`, `INTERACTION_UNKNOWN_VERB` |
| `synergy-markers.mjs` | Calibration markers | `injectJointMarkers(glbBuf,markers=DEFAULT_MARKERS,size=0.05)` — SKINNED tetra markers that ride bones; per-marker `offset:[x,y,z]` floats it off the joint (offset rotates WITH the bone); markers include NORMAL (PP drops normal-less primitives) · `validateMarkers(glbBuf)` |
| `kinetic-transplant.mjs` | In-process clip swap | `parseGlb(buf)→{json,bin}` / `serializeGlb(json,bin)` (append-only, 4-byte aligned) · `transplantKinetic(charBuf,libBuf,slugs)→{glb,slugToAnimId,report,clips,healed}` (bone-name channel copy + bind-pose quaternion retarget + auto-HEALS zero-length bufferViews) · `computeContacts(glbBuf)→{slug:{contactMs,durationMs}}` · `deriveIntentClipMap(slugs,intentMap)` · `DEFAULT_KINETIC_INTENTS` (`PLACEHOLDER_PNG` is private, not an export) |
| `presenter-director.mjs` | Per-slide performance | `resolvePresenter(specPresenter,worldPresenter,avatarClipMap,avatarContactMap,avatarClipDurations)` (merge DEFAULT←world←author; fields incl. `scale` [0.5–2.2], `viewportMargin` [1–3, DIVIDES; values in (2,3] are OUTSIDE the measured L8 envelope m∈[1,2]], `faceRotationY` default 180, `travelPath`, `pairContent`, `contactMap`, `clipDurations`) · `expandPresenterDirective(spec,options)` — single presenter/slide, gesture-from-narration (L2 keyword classifier `classifyGesture`), SQUARE windows via `placement()` · `expandCastDirective(spec,{castAssets})` — multi-actor scenes: `presenters.cast[]` + `slides[].scene[]` beats `{actor,intent?,animId?,at,bbox?}`, where `at` accepts `withPrevious`, `afterPrevious`, or milliseconds; objectName per beat, morphName=actorId (one Morph thread per actor) · `planPresenterGestures` |
| `choreography-engine.mjs` | Deterministic validate/predict/compile | `CLIP_CEILING=18` · `validate(ir)` (codes incl. `MULTI_3D_SCENE`, `CLIP_CEILING`, `CONTENT_ZONE`) · `compile(ir)` emits wire-format cues (`duration`/`delay` keys, never `*Ms`) · current `pathProps()` emits only `motionPathData` (known T9 release defect) |
| `spec-compiler.mjs` | The deck compiler | Current order is `materializeKineticForSpec` → preliminary `planDeckLayout`/`interactionTargetBoxes` → `expandInteractions` → `expandCastDirective`/`expandPresenterDirective` → one bounded final layout. Meaning-only targets therefore receive layout boxes before solving; authored `bbox` remains an explicit override. `castAssetsForSpec(spec,withBytes)` decodes actor GLBs + pulls `clipTimelines` from the process-local cache. Response: `appliedDefaults.presenter.gestures` + `.interactions` (reuses `spec._synergyPlan`; T12). `shapeOptions` reads **`style.fill`** (T11). |

### 2.2 Asset provider (`betterpptx-mcp/assets/model3d-gen-providers.mjs`)

**Implementation correction (2026-08-01):** the former `spec-compiler` order debt is retired locally. Current code
performs a preliminary `planDeckLayout`/`interactionTargetBoxes` pass using the declared slide size, solves
interactions, then performs one bounded final layout. Authored `bbox` is an override; it is not the normal
meaning-only path. The fresh A3 replay reports M4 `4/4` solved and `4/4` layout-derived boxes; the cold
determinism verifier is **18/18** in the development aggregate. Remaining gaps are release evidence (clean
provenance, owner approvals, and dedicated v3 assets), not the local order-swap implementation.

**Production boundary (2026-07-19):** this self-hosted TRELLIS.2 + DRTK + UniRig provider is retained
for PoC/flow testing only. Its permissive upstream metadata must not be interpreted as production or
resale clearance. Personalized production native 3D is exclusively the ordered multi-view
**Meshy → Mixamo** route enforced by `betterpptx-mcp/ir/native3d-policy.mjs`.

- `registerLocalRigged3d({glbBase64,glbBytes,previewBase64,clipMap,contacts,clipTimelines,license,basisKey}) → {handle:'gen://trellis/rigged3d/<hash>',hash}` — seeds a prebuilt avatar into the **process-local** gen cache.
- `materializeKineticPresenter({baseGlbBase64,baseHash,slugs,intentMap,previewBase64,license})` — process-local custom-≤18 kinetic swap (~114–400 ms); idempotent by hash only within the surviving cache. It does **not** cache subset-specific `clipTimelines`, so the resulting actor is not interaction-ready and currently receives `INTERACTION_NO_TIMELINES` until A1 produces/attaches them.
- `getCachedGeneratedRigged3d(hash)` → cache entry (see §4.3). `parseGenRigged3dHandle(handle)`.
- The current cache contract does not carry a complete versioned calibration/oracle manifest; registration can
  carry timelines, but worker output does not yet produce or durably attach them.
- Kinetic library at `betterpptx-mcp/assets/kinetic-clips.glb` (3.25 MB, 31 clips) is TRACKED as of
  2026-07-14 (`62bed4ca6`) — a git-based build now includes it. Its G5 distribution record is pending
  owner approval (Mixamo-derived skeletal tracks).

Current distinction: the local adapter may register `clipTimelines`, while the durable generated-asset store
(`betterpptx-mcp/storage/gen-asset-store.mjs`) is the intended restart/cross-instance authority. Personalized
production routing is not this TRELLIS/UniRig adapter; it is the owner-gated Meshy → Mixamo policy in
`betterpptx-mcp/ir/native3d-policy.mjs`.

  `$env:PYTHONUTF8='1'` in PowerShell / `PYTHONUTF8=1` in process environments (T14) · Node `24.11.1` ESM.

  with env (see §5.1). MCP tool entry: `engine.callBetterPptxTool(name, args)`.

  env `BETTERPPTX_TTS_PROVIDER=piper-http`, `BETTERPPTX_TTS_URL=http://127.0.0.1:5000`. ~26 s/clip on CPU —
  raise `BETTERPPTX_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 120000) for narrated decks.

### 2.6 Binding compiler contract (target architecture)

The current pre-layout interaction expansion is a prototype constraint, not the product contract. Implement this
single bounded pipeline; do not grow a second independent planner in the presenter, interaction, or layout layer:

1. Normalize and validate the meaning-level spec, versioned manifests, slide size, execution profile, motion
   policy, and seed.
2. Materialize/resolve actors and build the semantic object registry without reading arbitrary local paths.
3. Compute narration/media durations and lower author intent into a shared **Timeline IR**. Narration is the
   master clock; gestures, interactions, scenes, object effects, audio, and transitions are tracks on it.
4. Run preliminary layout and stage reservation using actual `slideW`/`slideH`; assign target boxes and a declared
   target envelope. Authored `bbox` is an optional constraint/override, never a normal prerequisite.
5. Solve actor placement, semantic clip choice, contact window, and object effect against those boxes and the
   actor's hash-keyed oracle manifest.
6. Feed solver constraints back to layout and allow **one deterministic replan**. If still infeasible, preserve
   the content and emit an actionable degradation/refusal; never loop or silently move authored content.
7. Suppress a redundant default presenter beat when the same actor already owns an interaction/scene beat for
   that slide. Compile every accepted track into native animations from the final Timeline IR.
8. Validate choreography, package topology, relationships/content types, black-box package privacy, size, and
   execution-profile support; then emit the deck plus one canonical storyboard.

Every dimension calculation must derive from the declared slide size; `13.333 × 7.5` constants are defects. Every
planner decision uses `{code,severity,reason,fix,evidence}`. Unknown/unmeasured behavior degrades explicitly. The
compiler is deterministic for `(normalizedSpec, executionProfile, motionPolicy, seed, manifest hashes, compiler
version)`; human/perceptual and PowerPoint-runtime gates remain separate evidence classes.

## 3. LOCAL MEASURED LAWS (legacy `PP365-16.0.x`, Windows desktop, CreateVideo oracle)

L1–L10 are strong local observations from the July research artifacts, not yet release baselines. Their recorded
build label is generic, so A1/A3 must reproduce them against an exact PowerPoint version/build and hash-keyed
artifact before they can gate production. Re-verify when any execution-profile component changes: PowerPoint
build/channel/platform, compiler duration rule, avatar/library bytes, poster/camera conditions, or oracle version.
Do not generalize CreateVideo behavior to live slideshow, edit/save round-trips, Mac, web, or mobile; A4a/A4b/G6 own that
matrix. Encode a law only inside its measured envelope and preserve an explicit fallback outside it.

| # | Law | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | The live am3d render is **UNIFORM** (isotropic). The graphicFrame is a clipping **WINDOW** — it never stretches the model. | Aspect distortion is impossible in the live render. Windows can be any shape; SQUARE is used so the poster doesn't stretch (L5). |
| L2 | `objViewport viewportSz = frameHeightIn × 1454545 / margin` renders the mesh rest-height exactly `frameHeight/margin` inches. Larger viewportSz = more zoomed IN. | The constant lives in `synergy-geometry.REST_FIT_VIEWPORT_PER_INCH` and is hard-coded at the director/compiler emit sites. |
| L3 | Anchor: mesh rest-bbox CENTER ↦ frame center (both axes). | `modelToSlide` / inverse-placement algebra. |
| L4 | The PowerPoint **camera** `rotateY:180` (face-audience) does **NOT** mirror X on the slide; no measurable perspective (kPz=0, affine). Small anchor bias kX=−0.097, kY=+0.069 in (joe-slim; kinetic-joe measured −0.2616/−0.0222 — the bias is ACTOR/POSE-specific, use the actor's own manifest). **ENVELOPE (measured 2026-07-14): kPz=0 holds only for STANDING, depth-compact poses (marker zSpread ≤ ~0.35 u) — a lying/plank pose put z-extreme markers >1 in off while hips (z≈0) stayed exact. The depth term is real but unmeasured.** Interaction placement is currently calibrated only for the actor `faceRotationY:0` front-facing roster basis; non-zero actor yaws are unmeasured and T31 refuses them. | `DEFAULT_CALIBRATION` + per-actor oracle manifests. Keep the camera's 180° visual convention distinct from the actor's measured interaction basis. Calibration probes must use a standing plateau pose (enforced in `oracle-run.mjs`). |
| L5 | The POSTER raster IS stretched to the frame in static contexts (exports/first paint/thumbnails). | Square windows + square posters. The poster is the designed static fallback. |
| L6 | `playScene` selects clip via attrName `embedded{animId+1}`; **18-clip ceiling** (animId ≥ 18 silently static); **one 3D-scene animation per model INSTANCE** (chaining/3D-emphasis on the same spid → static). A 2D motion path CAN co-fire on the same instance. | Scenes = multiple instances. Library cap ≤18. |
| L7 | **`fill=hold` does NOT hold am3d scene time.** After the playScene node ends the model BLENDS BACK TO REST (~400 ms). During play: near-native rate early, then compressed to finish within the node duration; per-clip start latency varies. | Mid-clip freezes DO NOT EXIST. Never model the time-warp — use empirical timelines (§4.2). Play full clip length; target pose PLATEAUS. |
| L8 | Windows with h < ~3.5 in hit PP's auto-fit floor (render larger than L2 predicts, +0.5 in effector error). A GLB with NO animations changes the zoom law entirely (auto-fit; margins ignored). | Solver lattice restricted to h∈[3.5,6.5], m∈[1,2]. Probes must run PRODUCTION conditions (animations present). |
| L9 | Invalid glTF (a zero-length bufferView; a primitive without NORMAL) → PP silently refuses to play ALL animation / silently drops the primitive. | Gates in slim/transplant/markers already enforce this. Never emit either. |
| L10 | animMotion custom paths: slide-fraction relative `"M 0 0 L x y … E"`, multi-L supported, traversed with hard-coded `accel=50000 decel=50000` easing (`dist/node/gen-animations-builder.js:505`). | Carry endpoint is exact; mid-path has easing skew until A2b ships the pass-through. |

## 4. DATA CONTRACTS (target schemas with legacy local measurements)

### 4.1 Character execution manifest v2 (per actor/library/profile; produced by A1)
```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 2,
  "oracleVersion": "<git-sha-or-tool-hash>",
  "actorHash": "sha256:<glb>",
  "clipLibraryHash": "sha256:<library>",
  "compilerVersion": "<private-commit>",
  "executionProfile": {
    "platform": "windows-desktop",
    "surface": "create-video",
    "versionToReport": "<exact-at-measurement>",
    "clientVersionToReport": "<exact-at-measurement>"
  },
  "calibration": {
    "kX": -0.097, "kY": 0.069, "kS": 1.0, "kPz": 0.0, "mirrorX": false,
    "restFitViewportPerInch": 1454545,
    "fitConditions": ["c0 h5 m1 center", "c2 h5 m2 center"],
    "heldOutMedianIn": 0.102, "allMedianIn": 0.102
  },
  "measuredAt": "<ISO-8601>",
  "evidence": { "artifactHash": "sha256:<probe-deck>", "resultHash": "sha256:<report>" }
}
```
The numeric values above are legacy local measurements. A release manifest is valid only when every placeholder
is concrete, the hashes match the exact bytes being served, and the declared profile has a green A1/A3 result.

### 4.2 Empirical clip-timeline manifest — production timing source
```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 2,
  "actorHash": "sha256:<glb>",
  "clipLibraryHash": "sha256:<library>",
  "executionProfileId": "<hash-of-4.1-profile>",
  "oracleVersion": "<git-sha-or-tool-hash>",
  "durationRule": { "kind": "min", "clipMsCap": 4500 },
  "effector": "mixamorig:RightHand",
  "probe": { "slideW": 13.333, "slideH": 7.5, "x": 4.17, "y": 1.8, "w": 5.0, "h": 5.0, "m": 1 },
  "semanticClips": {
    "point-at": [3], "touch": [4], "press": [5],
    "push": [6], "carry": [7, 8], "knock-over": [9]
  },
  "timelines": { "3": [[0, 5.97, 3.82], [33, 5.97, 3.82]] },
  "evidence": { "artifactHash": "sha256:<probe-deck>", "resultHash": "sha256:<timeline-report>" }
}
```
`timelines[animId]` = `[videoMs, effectorX_in, effectorY_in]` of the RightHand marker rendered in the probe
window, ~30 Hz, per clip, playScene duration = min(clipLen, 4500). Convert to model space via the affine
inverse of `modelToSlide` using the probe window as ctx. This manifest ABSORBS every L7 quirk. Produce with:
`clip-timeline-probe.mjs` (build deck) → COM render (§5.2) → the extraction script in the same folder.
**Timing validity is tied to the emitted duration rule and the full execution-profile/hash tuple.** A semantic
verb may select only clips declared for that intent (or an explicit authored override); scanning every animation
for the nearest hand trajectory can produce physically plausible but narratively wrong choices.

### 4.3 Generated-asset record (current cache and required durable contract)
Keys the executor reads: `glbBase64, glbBytes, previewBase64 (square transparent poster), clipMap
(intent→animId), contacts ([{animId,contactMs,durationMs}]), clipTimelines (§4.2), license, manifest`.
`registerLocalRigged3d` fills the byte/poster/clip/contact/timeline fields for a prebuilt avatar, but its current
`manifest` is not the complete schema-v2 calibration/provenance contract in §4.1. The auto-poster wiring attaches
`previewBase64` as the am3d raster at embed time — model3d blocks referencing a cached handle need no
explicit `posterAsset`.

Today this record lives in a process-local `Map`; its handle can fail after restart or on another instance. F1/G3
must replace that assumption with tenant-scoped durable object storage plus metadata/index records, immutable
content hashes, authorization checks, idempotent materialization, lifecycle/retention, and cross-instance reads.
Calibration, timelines, license evidence, and source provenance travel with the asset as versioned hash-keyed
manifests. Public results return only opaque product handles/download URLs, never provider names or storage paths.

### 4.4 The authoring surfaces (what the client AI writes)
```json
{
  "authoringContract": { "workflow": "authoring-excellence-director-v1" },
  "contentStory": { "audience": "executive audience", "outcome": "make the key message memorable", "remember": "one clear takeaway per slide" },
  "visualDesign": { "world": "calibrated presentation stage", "framing": "content-first with a clear presenter window" },
  "motionTime": { "masterClock": "authored slide timeline", "timingSource": "narration and measured interaction timelines" },
  "executionProfile": "windows-desktop",
  "motionPolicy": "full",
  "seed": "stable-author-seed",
  "presenters": {
    "cast": [
      {
        "id": "joe",
        "model": "<opaque-model-handle>",
        "kinetic": { "clips": ["greet", "point_high_right"] },
        "faceRotationY": 0
      }
    ]
  },
  "slides": [
    { "id": "s1", "scene": [{ "actor": "joe", "intent": "greet", "at": "afterPrevious" }] },
    {
      "id": "s2",
      "interactions": [{ "actor": "joe", "verb": "point-at", "target": "metric-card", "distIn": 2.2 }]
    }
  ]
}
```
Current authoring contract: the engine assigns boxes to meaning-only targets, and authored `bbox`/`rect`/`x`/`y`/`w`/`h`
are forbidden in Director composition. The engine's measured rectangles are readback proposals, not author inputs;
the interaction solver accepts only the measured `faceRotationY:0` roster basis. Actors are
normally declared in `presenters.cast` (a lone top-level `presenter` is normalized into a cast-of-one), and
`presenters.onSlides:'none'` can suppress the default cast beat when an interaction owns the slide. Accepted
interaction/scene beats suppress duplicate defaults, and an authored target animation is preserved or rejected
with an actionable conflict — never silently overwritten. Remaining limitations are dedicated v3 source/manifest
coverage and external release/perceptual evidence, not a hidden coordinate prerequisite.

### 4.5 The plan the AI reads back (response `appliedDefaults.presenter.interactions`)
```json
{ "slide": 2, "slideId": "s2", "actor": "joe", "verb": "push", "target": "metric",
  "clip": 5, "contactMs": 2640, "dir": {"x": 0.026, "y": -1}, "speedInPerS": 16.84,
  "framePos": {"x": 7.844, "y": 2.329}, "clampedIn": 0, "verdicts": [] }
```
Contact verbs add `window:[startMs,endMs]`, `effectorSlide`, `residualIn`, the profile/manifest identifiers used,
and evidence references. Every decision/degradation/refusal has `{code,severity,reason,fix,evidence}`. A skipped
beat remains in the canonical storyboard with its nearest feasible placement/clip and a concrete author action.

## 5. STANDARD RECIPES (copy these; they encode a day of debugging each)

### 5.1 Build a deck through the local engine (development harness only)
```js
import path from 'node:path'; import * as os from 'node:os';
Object.assign(process.env, { BETTERPPTX_ASSETS_ROOT:'D:/PptxGenJS_Claude',
  BETTERPPTX_ALLOW_PRIMITIVE_TOOLS:'1', BETTERPPTX_FREE_PREVIEW_SLIDES:'99',
  BETTERPPTX_DEV_ACCEPT_ANY_ACTIVATION_KEY:'1', BETTERPPTX_BILLING_ENFORCE:'0',
  BETTERPPTX_TOOL_TIMEOUT_MS:'600000',
  BETTERPPTX_CREDENTIAL_STORE: path.join(os.tmpdir(), `bpx-${process.pid}.json`) });
const engine = await import('file:///D:/PptxGenJS_Claude/betterpptx-mcp/engine.mjs');
const gen = await engine.callBetterPptxTool('betterpptx.deck.generate_from_spec',
  { spec, phase:'commit', plan:'free', detail:'full' });
if (!gen?.ok) throw new Error(JSON.stringify(gen?.error));
const data = gen?.data ?? gen;
const deckId = data?.deckId;
if (!deckId) throw new Error('generate_from_spec returned no deckId');
// details.errors carries codes like OFF_SLIDE_OBJECT with rects
await engine.callBetterPptxTool('betterpptx.deck.save', { deckId, fileName, outputDir });
```
`BETTERPPTX_DEV_ACCEPT_ANY_ACTIVATION_KEY=1`, billing disabled, primitive-tool access, local paths, and an
ephemeral credential store make this a maintainer harness. Its success is not entitlement, billing, black-box,
durability, or deployed-product evidence. Never use these bypasses in a release gate or public service.

### 5.2 COM render (PowerShell metrology oracle — CreateVideo renders live 3D, Slide.Export shows the poster)
```powershell
$pp=New-Object -ComObject PowerPoint.Application
$ppVersion=$pp.Version                         # also record exact Click-to-Run VersionToReport/client build
$deck=$pp.Presentations.Open($pptx,$true,$false,$false)      # readonly, no window
$deck.CreateVideo($mp4,$true,5,1080,30,85)                    # UseTimings, 5s/slide, 1080p30
for($t=0;$t -lt 300;$t++){Start-Sleep 2; if($deck.CreateVideoStatus -eq 3){break}}  # 3=done
Start-Sleep 4; $deck.Close(); $pp.Quit()
[System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($pp)|Out-Null
```
Slides land at uniform segments in the video (`seg = N/nSlides` frames). Sample with imageio-ffmpeg (`py`,
`imageio.v2.get_reader(mp4,'ffmpeg')` — pyav is NOT installed).

### 5.3 Measure (Python)
- Hue-keyed markers/objects: `research-demos/2026-07-13-synergy-calibration/measure_pixels.py`
  (`markers|silhouette` modes) or inline HSV: sat>0.45, val>0.35, hue tol ±0.05, median-centered cluster.
  SAFE HUES against Joe: red/green/magenta/cyan (yellow≈skin, blue≈navy suit). Cards: `FF6600` orange
  (sat>0.6, ≥600 px blob).
- **Truth discipline: for 3D self-animation, only FILMSTRIPS count** (pixel-motion metrics cannot tell frozen
  from animating — proven twice). For contact accuracy under occlusion use pre/post-contact MEDIANS
  (naive centroid tracking is poisoned by the presenter's arm crossing the target).

### 5.4 The probe loop (any new PP-behavior question)
build a minimal deck varying ONE parameter across slides (§5.1) → render (§5.2) → measure (§5.3) → write the
law into §3 with the exact numbers → encode it in code with the law number in a comment.
**Probes MUST run production conditions** (animations present + explicit playScene; L8).

### 5.5 Local-R&D Modal deploy (both profiles; not release evidence)
```powershell
Set-Location D:\PptxGenJS_Claude\betterpptx-mcp\deploy\model3d-worker
$env:PYTHONUTF8='1'; [Console]::OutputEncoding=[System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8   # else the CLI crashes on '✓'
foreach($p in @('betterpptx','betterxavatar')){ $env:MODAL_PROFILE=$p; py -m modal deploy model3d_app.py }
```
This deploys the current local worker tree and bypasses immutable-source, owner-secret, licensing, canary, and
rollback controls. Use only for maintainer experiments with owner-approved spend. It is forbidden as F1/P5
evidence; G2a/G4/G5 must first produce and verify an immutable worker deployment path.

### 5.6 Local presenter-director composition self-test
```bash
node D:/PptxGenJS_Claude/research-demos/2026-07-12-presenter-cofire/verify-director-compose.mjs  # expect 24/24
```
This verifier exercises presenter-director composition assertions only; it does not cover the interaction solver,
saved package, PowerPoint runtime, or end-to-end generation. Run it after director changes, then run A3/G gates
for compiler/solver/release evidence.

### 5.7 Immutable private MCP canary (after explicit pre-canary gates are green)
```powershell
Set-Location D:\PptxGenJS_Claude
$dirty = git status --porcelain=v1 --untracked-files=all
if ($dirty) { throw 'Canary source must be a clean, fully tracked private worktree.' }
$sha = (git rev-parse HEAD).Trim()
.\deploy\gcp\build-mcp-metrology-from-git.ps1 `
  -GitSourceRevision $sha -GitCommit $sha -GitTreeClean
# Use the image@sha256 digest printed by the build, never a mutable tag or the dirty local tree:
.\deploy\gcp\deploy-mcp-metrology.ps1 `
  -PrebuiltImage '<registry/image@sha256:digest>' `
  -GcsBucket '<private-artifact-bucket>' `
  -TopologyRole production-gateway -MaxInstances 2
```
Also verify `$sha` exists on the approved private remote; the build script's `-GitTreeClean` is a recorded caller
assertion, not a cleanliness check. The script's default `-GitSourceUrl` is the **Developer Connect repository
resource** (`projects/…/connections/betterpptx-github/repositories/betterpptx-mcp`) — a plain https:// GitHub URL
cannot fetch this private repo (anonymous git fetch fails; measured 2026-07-14). `-TopologyRole` is required here: the script's default `serial-baseline`
envelope enforces `MaxInstances=1` and rejects this call (`deploy-mcp-metrology.ps1:36-48`). `MaxInstances=2`
permits the controlled G3 concurrency probe but does not by itself prove that two instances served it; verify
that privately through the test harness/maintainer logs.
The deploy script owns service configuration, health checks, and provenance rules. Do not substitute a raw
`gcloud run services update`. The container currently defaults to `ARG BUILD_DIST=1`, so the git revision must
contain every source/module/asset required to reproduce the build; compare source and built-artifact hashes.

### 5.8 Saved-package and public-surface release scan

For every release fixture: parse every XML and `.rels`; resolve every internal relationship; validate content
type overrides; inspect package topology; run the convergence comparator where shared serialization changed; and
open/edit/save/reopen in the applicable PowerPoint profile. Then scan public schemas, help, results, errors,
health, download metadata, and **decoded** BetterPPTX-owned package sidecars for repo paths, source/process details,
dependency/provider/model/host names, internal handle schemes, tenant data, tokens, and secrets. Compression or
base64 does not exempt a payload. G1 is red until the scanner decodes the current edition sidecar and the public
3D-generation vocabulary/handles have product-generic replacements.

## 6. TRAP COMPENDIUM (each cost ≥1 render cycle; do not re-pay)

| # | Trap | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| T1 | Zero-length bufferView (e.g. from dropping an image) → PP freezes ALL animation, silently | 1×1 PNG placeholder; gates already throw (`gltf_ops.GateError`, transplant/markers output checks) |
| T2 | Primitive without NORMAL → PP silently doesn't render it | markers/injected geometry must include NORMAL |
| T3 | Texture "unused" scans missing EXTENSION texture refs (KHR_materials_specular) → referenced map nuked | generic `*Texture` walk over material extensions (in `slim_textures`) |
| T4 | `fill=hold` on playScene ≠ held pose (L7) | empirical timelines; plateaus; full-clip durations |
| T5 | GLB without animations → different zoom law (L8) | never probe/calibrate on anim-stripped models |
| T6 | Small windows (h<3.5) break the viewport law (L8) | solver lattice = calibrated envelope only |
| T7 | Assuming rotY180 mirrors X | it does NOT (L4) — hands swap if you mirror |
| T8 | `*Ms` timing keys (`durationMs`/`delayMs`) on animation objects | engine reads `duration`/`delay` (ms); *Ms silently ignored → family defaults apply (500 ms emphasis/most, 2000 ms motion paths, delay 0) |
| T9 | Custom path via `motionPathData` only | the builder reads `pathData`/`path`; emit validated `pathData` (plus a compatibility alias only where required) or the default path/delay can ship. **FIXED 2026-07-14:** `choreography-engine.pathProps()` now emits BOTH keys; `timeline-ir.wireCue()` enforces it for every new lowering path (tested in `verify-timeline-ir.mjs`). |
| T10 | Motion-path names that are exit/emphasis effects ("curveDown") | class-crossing coerces visibly; use presets from the registry or custom pathData |
| T11 | `fill:`/`line:` at the top level of a `kind:'shape'` spec object | silently ignored — must be under `style:{}` |
| T12 | Re-solving the interaction plan on the EXPANDED spec | every beat mis-reports `TARGET_AUTHORED`; reuse `spec._synergyPlan` |
| T13 | Shifting a solved frame after clamping (exact-correction) | re-clamp + surface the loss (`FRAME_CLAMPED`), else `OFF_SLIDE_OBJECT` compile error |
| T14 | cp1252 console + Unicode (✓, Δ) in Python/Modal output | `PYTHONUTF8=1` everywhere; PowerShell `[Console]::OutputEncoding=UTF8` |
| T15 | Assuming deployment always consumes committed `dist/` | **OWNER-DECIDED 2026-07-14 (clarified): COMMITTED-DIST is the contract — `dist` is built LOCALLY on the dev host (`gulp ship`), committed, and the cloud image is an EXACT MIRROR (`BUILD_DIST=0`, no in-cloud rebuild).** `BETTERPPTX_IMPORT_DIST_DIRECT` stays `0` (direct import of the `.js` ESM bundle crashes init; the shadow-copy loader is load-time necessity). Verify `.dockerignore`/`.gcloudignore` actually ship `dist/`, pin the private git SHA, and compare image-dist hashes against the committed dist. |
| T16 | New tools registered in only one surface, or killed by the wrapper | update handler/tool registration, primitive application path where applicable, public schema/help, black-box allowlist/refusal tests, billing/entitlement/metering, and `LONG_RUNNING_TOOLS`; exercise through a cold public client. |
| T17 | PP COM `Add3DModel` accepts GLB only (FBX is UI-converter-only) | convert via `fbx_to_glb.py` (Blender, worker) |
| T18 | Matte look is OWNER-CHOSEN (anti-"glassy" fix); 512 caps are OWNER-CHOSEN | don't "improve" either without asking |
| T19 | pixel-motion metrics for 3D animation truth | filmstrips only (§5.3) |
| T20 | `assembleClickGroups` re-buckets withPrevious delays in some flows | verify final delays in the slide XML (`<p:cond delay=`) when timing matters |
| T21 | Treating compressed/base64 sidecar data as private | decode and scan every BetterPPTX-owned package part. The current edition sidecar can reveal provider/host/generator fields and internal handles. |
| T22 | Treating `gen://…` as durable because it is hash-shaped | the current generated-asset cache is a process-local `Map`; prove restart, cross-instance, tenant isolation, authorization, and idempotency against durable storage. |
| T23 | Deploying from git while required modules/assets are untracked | remote builds cannot include the local Synergy modules/kinetic library. Freeze reviewed files, provenance, and hashes in a private commit before a canary. |
| T24 | Hard-coded `13.333 × 7.5` slide math | derive all stage, solver, effect, path, and layout fractions from `slideW`/`slideH`; regression-test wide, standard, vertical, and square profiles. |
| T25 | Choosing an interaction clip from every timeline | filter by the versioned semantic clip map first; the nearest hand path may belong to a semantically wrong clip. |
| T26 | Using local bypass flags as product proof | `DEV_ACCEPT_ANY_ACTIVATION_KEY`, billing-off, primitive access, and local file paths are local-harness only; release evidence comes from a cold entitled client. |
| T27 | Solving interactions before target layout | preliminary layout must assign boxes, then solve, then allow one bounded replan (§2.6). Hand-authored bboxes are overrides, not the product path. |
| T28 | Expecting a lone top-level `presenter` to satisfy interactions | normalize it into the actor/cast IR or emit a fix; current interaction expansion reads cast assets and can duplicate default beats. |
| T29 | Believing `tolIn=0.15` is enforced because it is an argument | current solver acceptance uses a different residual cutoff; make tolerance an explicit per-verb/profile contract and test the boundary. |
| T30 | Separating generated bytes from their oracle/license manifests | persist actor, poster, timelines, calibration, semantic map, provenance, and license evidence as one versioned hash-linked asset record. |
| T31 | Authoring non-zero `faceRotationY` as if interaction placement math supports it | current geometry ignores actor yaw while the compiler can emit it. The interaction compiler accepts only the measured 0° roster basis; reject/degrade any non-zero yaw or implement and remeasure a yaw-aware projection/profile before solving. |
| T32 | Treating a historical green artifact or generated status snapshot as current | record the source tree/commit and artifact timestamp; rebuild after source changes, then rerun comparator, package topology, PowerPoint repair/open, and visual oracle. |
| T33 | Running a compiler verifier through the wrong safety boundary | the current cold determinism child requires a running MCP endpoint; make the verifier endpoint-aware or provide a safe offline mode, and never convert an endpoint error into a digest claim. |
| T34 | Treating the ordinary gesture library as authoring-interaction proof | the dedicated `authoring-clipmap-v3` gate must be `SHIPPABLE` with all 18 measured timelines; `TO_SOURCE` rows are an intentional fail-closed result. |

## 7. THE WORKSTREAMS (executor detail)

Each item names its gate classes from §0.1. A gate is evidence, not a checkbox: record the command, threshold,
artifact/result hashes, exact private commit, profile/build, and result in the evidence ledger.

### A. Calculation Foundation

**A1. Render Oracle v1 — character-manifest production as a pipeline step**  
Gates: **METROLOGY · DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE**
- Files: NEW `tools/render-oracle/` (this repo): `oracle-run.mjs` (build a probe deck from an actor asset record:
  marker-inject via `synergy-markers.mjs`, one slide per clip, §4.2 duration rule), `oracle-render.ps1` (§5.2),
  `oracle-extract.py` (§5.3 → timelines JSON), `oracle-validate.mjs` (marker-probe vs `modelToSlide`, fits/refreshes
  calibration.json per execution profile). Record COM `$pp.Version` plus exact Click-to-Run
  `VersionToReport`/`ClientVersionToReport`; do not invent a generic build label.
- Approach: generalize the existing scripts in `research-demos/2026-07-13-synergy-calibration/` (build-probe3,
  clip-timeline-probe, marker-probe3, the extraction inline scripts) into parameterized tools keyed by
  `(actorHash, clipLibraryHash, executionProfile, oracleVersion)`. Store large evidence in the private hash-keyed
  artifact store, commit the small manifest/index required for reproduction, and attach the immutable manifest to
  the durable asset record. `registerLocalRigged3d({clipTimelines})` remains a local adapter, not authority.
- Gate: one command produces schema-v2 calibration + semantic timelines for kinetic-joe and baked-joe, held-out
  median ≤0.15 in, with the exact profile/build and all byte/tool/result hashes; a repeated run is idempotent.

**A2a. Timeline IR + compiler-order foundation**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE**
- Files: NEW `ir/timeline-ir.mjs`; `ir/spec-compiler.mjs`, `ir/layout-engine.mjs`, `ir/synergy-compiler.mjs`,
  `ir/presenter-director.mjs`, `ir/synergy-effects.mjs`, schemas and compiler tests.
- Approach: implement §2.6. Narration/media duration is computed before interactions; preliminary layout creates
  target boxes; actor/semantic clip/contact solving follows; one bounded replan is allowed; final native cues are
  emitted from the shared Timeline IR. Normalize a lone presenter into actor IR, suppress duplicate default beats,
  use actual slide dimensions, apply declared tolerances, reject/degrade unmeasured yaw, fix motionPathData-only
  compilation, and replace the fixed dwell constants (the 5000 ms interaction budget in `synergy-compiler.mjs`
  and the separate 4000 ms nominal `SLIDE_MS` in `presenter-director.mjs`) with the available slide/narration
  budget.
- Gate: fixed adversarial cases plus a seeded/versioned corpus across wide, standard, vertical, and square decks;
  stable plan and **canonical OOXML digests** on repeat (normalize ZIP timestamps and declared volatile metadata;
  retain the raw file hash only as evidence identity); M4 regenerated from meaning-only input without manual
  target bboxes or `onSlides:'none'`; zero duplicate actor beats; every refusal has the §1 decision shape. For
  unmeasured non-wide profiles, P1 proves dimension propagation/package validity only and emits reduced/static
  behavior until A1/A4a/A4b provides the profile's playback metrology.

**A2b. Core animation linearity pass (source + built artifact)**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PACKAGE**
- Files: pptxgenjs core source for `gen-animations-builder`, animation normalizer, and
  `src/core-interfaces.ts` (`AnimationProps`); `synergy-effects.mjs`; `dist/`; container-build verification.
- Approach: start only after G0 is fully green. Pass through validated `accel`/`decel` (0–100000), preserving the
  current default; carry emits `accel:0,decel:0`. Build exactly as the pinned image does and retain the before/after
  convergence evidence.
- Gate: M3 carry whole-path deviation ≤0.2 in, package/PowerPoint repair-free, no new comparator/XML/visual
  difference on the frozen 40-slide convergence fixture, and source/built-artifact hashes tied to the image.

**A3. Regression runner on the oracle box**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Files: NEW `tools/render-oracle/regression.mjs` — replays M1 marker probe (held-out median ≤0.15 in); all six
  M2/M3 verbs (solver residual ≤0.15 in; measurement uncertainty ≤0.10 in reported separately; push ≤15° and
  ≤120 ms; carry endpoint and whole path ≤0.2 in); and M4 meaning-only compilation (all six verbs planned or an
  expected actionable refusal). Keep deterministic/package/metrology thresholds as separate fields, not sums.
- Gate: one command produces a green/red machine-readable report and evidence bundle; replay M1–M4, package
  integrity, deterministic planning, frozen convergence where applicable, and required filmstrips. Run before
  releases and after PowerPoint, oracle, serializer, compiler-duration, actor, or clip-library changes.

**A4a. Early platform-risk probe**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: before roster multiplication, test a minimal static + playScene + poster deck in Windows live
  slideshow versus CreateVideo and at least one available non-Windows/web surface. Record exact versions and any
  inability to test. Unmeasured profiles default to designed static output; no optimistic capability inheritance.
- Gate: a small evidence matrix and explicit continue/limit/stop product decision. Windows live-vs-export
  differences are quantified; every unverified profile has a safe declared fallback.

**A4b. Full execution-profile and cross-platform truth**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: one probe deck with static, playScene, interaction, audio, transition, and reduced-motion slides.
  Test Windows live slideshow and CreateVideo, Mac desktop, web, and mobile. For supported edit surfaces, perform
  open → play → edit → save → reopen and inspect package topology; record exact product versions and capabilities.
- Gate: a versioned capability matrix and an honest product/fallback statement for every profile. No platform is
  declared static or fully animated by assumption; full/reduced/static output is selected from measured support.

### B. The Performer

**B1. Clip Library v2 — designed for authoring (highest leverage)**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL · LICENSING**
- Spec of the set (≤18, the L6 ceiling): reach-touch at 3 heights × 2 sides (6) · point 2 heights (2) ·
  press/tap (1) · grab-carry-drop with ≥400 ms plateaus at grab AND drop (2: near/far) · walk loop + turn (2) ·
  breathing idle (1) · emphatic beat (1) · greet/bow (1) · agree/disagree nods (2). Every working pose held
  ≥400 ms (the plateau solver then lands everything); every clip returns toward rest at end (L7 blend looks
  natural).
- Sourcing: Mixamo where clips exist; else commissioned/owner-recorded mocap →
  `mixamo_bake.py` retarget path. Bake via the existing worker (`bake_kinetic_library` pattern +
  `reorder_animations` + `validate` against a frozen clipmap v3 JSON).
- Files: `betterpptx-mcp/deploy/model3d-worker/canonical/authoring-clipmap-v3.json` (frozen contract), bake configs,
  `DEFAULT_KINETIC_INTENTS`-style intent map for the new slugs.
- Gate: every M2/M3 threshold passes on a fixed adversarial grid plus seeded/versioned samples over the **declared
  admissible target envelope** for each slide/profile/window family. No `UNREACHABLE` inside that envelope; an
  outside target yields the nearest feasible fix. All 18 slot meanings and content hashes are frozen, and every
  source clip has a G5 distribution record before it enters a shippable asset.

**B2. Launch roster ≥4 (≥2 female); post-gate target 6–8**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL · LICENSING**
- Pipeline per character (ONE command; wraps existing pieces): FBX/gen source → `fbx_to_glb.py` (Blender,
  worker) → `transplantKinetic` (library v2) → `normalize_materials` → `slim_textures` (512) → poster render
  (square, transparent; `render_preview.py` film_transparent) → `computeContacts` → A1 oracle (timelines +
  calibration check) → G3 durable asset/catalog registration. `registerLocalRigged3d` is permitted only as a
  local test adapter. Candidates on disk already: josh, louise, remy FBX. Watch T17 and bind-pose retarget.
- Gate: ≥4 characters (≥2 female) each passes A3 with its own hash-keyed execution/license manifests, poster,
  F5a size/open/render budgets, and a blind roster-quality rubric; no character inherits another character's oracle.

**B3. Target-aware gaze within the 18-slot contract**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: extend FK/oracle metadata with head/neck forward vectors at the contact plateau; rank only the
  intent-compatible B1 clips by hand residual **and** gaze error. Author gaze into the reach/point families or
  reallocate B1 slots. Do not append five micro-clips beyond 18, and do not assume a second gaze clip can layer on
  the same 3D instance (L6). If one library cannot cover a profile, publish a distinct ≤18-clip library manifest.
- Gate: seeded target grid meets a declared gaze-angle threshold; blind side-by-side filmstrips show improved
  joint hand/eye attention without regressing M2/M3 contact thresholds.

**B4. Stride-true travel**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: walk-loop stride speed from FK (`fkJointTrajectories` foot displacement per cycle) → set co-fire
  `travelMs` so path speed = stride speed × window scale (`inchesPerUnit` from `modelToSlide`). Pure calculation
  in `presenter-director.mjs` `contentSafeTravel`/travel emit.
- Gate: measured stride/path speed error plus blind filmstrips at three window sizes and two travel directions;
  no visible foot slide and no off-slide/occlusion regression.

### C. The Director

**C1. Deck-level story arcs**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PERCEPTUAL**
- Files: NEW `ir/story-director.mjs`; consumed during meaning normalization before preliminary layout/Timeline IR.
- Approach: inputs = slide roles (opener/body/data/closer — already in specs), narration lengths (computed),
  interaction beats (authored/proposed). Output = per-slide presenter posture: {presence: full|recede|off,
  energy: 0..1 → choreography/scale/margin, entrance/exit slides, motif clip choices kept consistent}. Encode
  as adjustments to the existing scene/presenter directives (it PRODUCES specs, not new primitives).
- Gate: deterministic decisions for a frozen 10-slide narrated fixture; presenter recedes during dense-data
  slides and interacts at the climax; storyboard shows why; blind rubric sign-off against an arc-free baseline.

**C2. Proposed interactions (teamwork doctrine — proposals, never verdicts)**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PERCEPTUAL**
- Files: `ir/synergy-compiler.mjs` (+ a proposal pass), surfaced under
  `appliedDefaults.presenter.interactionProposals` with `confidence` + `reason` (the narration phrase).
- Approach: reuse the L2 classifier vocabulary + phrase patterns ("this chart shows", "look at", "we removed X")
  → candidate `{verb,target}` against A2a's **geometry-free semantic registry**; target geometry is resolved later.
  Never auto-inject: the AI copies a proposal into `slides[].interactions` to accept it.
- Gate: fixed labeled corpus plus held-out examples; ≥70% precision and ≥60% recall over labeled applicable
  opportunities at the declared threshold, zero auto-injections, stable results by seed/compiler version, and
  every proposal cites the phrase and semantic target match.

**C3. Layout↔stage co-design**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE · PERCEPTUAL**
- Files: `ir/layout-engine.mjs`, target-envelope metadata, and policy/property tests; consume A2a interfaces.
- Approach: A2a owns the pipeline, boxes, and one-replan mechanism. C3 extends its **layout policy**: reserve a
  stage zone from content density and profile,
  place interactive targets within B1's declared envelope when meaning/layout constraints permit, solve, then
  request the A2a replan once. Preserve authored geometry as a hard/soft constraint according to schema; a clamp
  remains a safety verdict, never an invisible post-layout correction.
- Gate: fixed edge cases plus ≥200 seeded/versioned property cases across slide sizes, dense content, multiple
  actors, missing targets, authored boxes, and conflicting animations. Zero off-slide objects or silent movement;
  ≥99% of cases labeled feasible by an independent constraint oracle/fixture annotation solve without clamp or
  occlusion; every remaining case is deterministic and actionable.

**C4. Scenes v2 (chaining + actor↔actor)**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: lower all beats to the shared Timeline IR. Multi-beat scenes use one model instance per playScene
  clip (L6); handoff times come from empirical contact windows and narration budget; same-actor instances may
  cross-fade only when package/runtime probes validate the result. Actor↔actor handoff is two ownership intervals
  on one object track, not two independent delay calculations.
- Gate: a 1-slide scene: A carries the badge to center, B takes it to the right; object tracks whichever hand
  holds it (endpoint ≤0.2 in each leg; filmstrip).

**C5. Camera as language (probe before grammar)**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Probe: two slides, same model, different `am3d:camera` pos/fov (the stage3d worlds already vary these),
  Morph transition between → does Morph interpolate the camera (cinematic push-in)? Also probe animating
  camera within a slide (likely static — L6-adjacent).
- Gate: exact-profile probe artifact, XML inspection, live/render filmstrip, and edit/save/reopen result. Add
  `cameraArc` only for profiles where the measured behavior is stable; otherwise return a static/reduced fallback.

### D. The Sensory Layer

**D1. SFX at solved contacts**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL · LICENSING**
- Files: `ir/synergy-effects.mjs` (`synthesizeEffect` gains an optional sfx cue), asset store (CC0 packs:
  whoosh/thud/pop/slide) with a G5-approved source/hash/license record and decoded-package-safe public terms.
- Approach: first probe native delayed audio and final XML/runtime behavior. If supported, compile an SFX track in
  the shared Timeline IR at `contactMs`; do not disguise SFX as narration. Volume/selection is world- and
  motion-policy-aware; static/reduced profiles remain intelligible without it.
- Gate: A/B render with/without SFX; sync within ±120 ms measured on the audio track (extract with ffmpeg,
  correlate onset vs contactMs).

**D2. Secondary motion**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: post-push settle = 2 chained short paths (overshoot + return, computed from the push vector);
  impact squash = growShrink 120 ms at contact; knock-over adds a small rotation emphasis before the exit.
  All synthesized in `synthesizeEffect` from existing contact geometry. Mind T8/T9/T20.
- Gate: filmstrip judgment + no timing regressions in A3.

**D3. Impact accents**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PACKAGE · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: native starburst/line shape, 200 ms entrance+exit at the solved contact point, world-styled and
  omitted/de-emphasized under reduced motion.
- Gate: fixed-position/package tests plus a blind rubric against no-accent and over-accent baselines.

**D4. Carry precision finish**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: after A2b, use linear acceleration and only then evaluate deterministic pre-warped sampling.
- Gate: ≤0.2 in whole-path deviation with occlusion-robust measurement and no visible jitter.

**D5. Full/reduced/static presentation policy**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · PERCEPTUAL**
- Approach: compile `motionPolicy` through every track. Reduced mode limits travel/impact/camera/SFX; static mode
  removes runtime dependence while preserving meaning through poster, layout, labels, notes/captions, and alt
  descriptions. Rubric: all required facts/labels/data remain visible; reading order is logical; meaningful
  non-decorative media has alt text; spoken-only information has captions/transcript; no trigger or sound is needed
  to understand the slide; reduced mode removes large travel/impact/camera motion. Never make the presenter the
  sole carrier of a fact or instruction.
- Gate: every flagship slide communicates its intended message with the actor hidden and under static output;
  reduced/static packages are repair-free, have zero critical accessibility-validator/rubric findings, and contain
  no orphaned triggers.

### E. The AI Cockpit

**E1. The canonical storyboard surface**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PERCEPTUAL**
- Files: `spec-compiler.mjs` (buildAppliedDefaults) — unify `presenter.gestures` + `presenter.interactions` +
  scene beats + narration timings + C1 arc into ONE `storyboard` object (per slide: what plays, when, why,
  verdicts). Dry-run: `phase:'plan'` already returns without committing.
- Gate: a cold external AI authors and adjusts a six-slide interacting deck using only public spec/help/storyboard;
  the deterministic plan predicts the final render within the declared rubric, with no maintainer vocabulary.

**E2. Actionable decisions/refusals**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC**
- Audit every decision across layout/synergy/director/choreography for `{code,severity,reason,fix,evidence}`.
  `UNREACHABLE`, for example, returns the nearest feasible box/window/semantic clip and quantified residual.
- Gate: schema validation plus fixed tests for every code; zero bare strings or warnings in plan/storyboard output.

**E3. Product-generic discovery**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- Add public capability/recipe/schema guidance for actors, scenes, interactions, motion policies, and opaque asset
  handles. Remove provider/model/host/dependency names and internal schemes from public tools/help/results/errors.
- Gate: a cold entitled MCP client discovers and uses interactions from help alone; an adversarial client cannot
  recover implementation, source, filesystem, process, provider, model, host, secret, or private-asset details.

### F. The Product

**F1. Immutable private canary (not public launch)**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- After **G0, G1, G2a, G3, G4, G5, E3, F3, and F5a** are green — plus, per the binding P4 order (§8),
  **G0-green A2b and the full A3 regression** have run: commit reviewed
  modules/assets/manifests privately; build with
  `deploy/gcp/build-mcp-metrology-from-git.ps1 -GitSourceRevision … -GitCommit … -GitTreeClean`; deploy the
  resulting digest with `deploy-mcp-metrology.ps1 -PrebuiltImage image@sha256:…`; retain the prior digest.
- Gate: cold entitled MCP client generates M4 end-to-end after service restart and a second-instance read; saved
  package/privacy scans pass; usage row and costs reconcile; canary rollback to the prior digest is demonstrated.
  Successful F1 evidence makes G2b green.

**F2. Character onboarding productized**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · LICENSING · OPERATIONS**
- Wrap B2 as one owner-only worker workflow plus durable catalog registration and a product-generic opaque handle.
- Gate: a licensed source becomes interaction-ready through one audited owner action, A1/A3 pass, then remains
  usable after restart/cross-instance without exposing provider/storage/source details.

**F3. Entitlements, metering, and billing reconciliation**  
Gates: **PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- Cover every new tool and implicit generation path in entitlement, estimator, usage ledger, rate card, quota,
  retry/idempotency, and refund/failure semantics. Keep free-preview policy owner-controlled, not copied by habit.
- Gate: allow/deny matrices pass; duplicate/retried calls do not double-charge; usage/cost/price reconcile; every
  billing-critical secret has the G4 owner-control path; public results expose no secret or internal cost topology.

**F4. Flagship release fixture**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · DETERMINISTIC · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL · PRIVACY · LICENSING · OPERATIONS**
- Narrated story, ≥2 diverse actors, ≥4 interaction verbs on real content, SFX, visual language, Morph continuity,
  and C1 arc from a meaning-only spec. Compile full/reduced/static variants; use it as an A3/G super-fixture.
- Gate: all applicable evidence classes green plus recorded owner approval. Approval makes a release candidate;
  only P5 makes it public.

**F5a. Performance/artifact budget contract and pilot guardrails**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · OPERATIONS**
- Freeze budgets per profile for input/output MB, model/media bytes, plan/generation latency (warm/cold), open/save,
  render, memory, worker time, and estimated/actual cloud cost. Define warning/refusal/degradation thresholds and
  a fail-closed owner spend preflight/alert for the binding Modal cap of ≤$30/month/profile.
- Gate: owner approves concrete thresholds before B2 roster multiplication; one pilot actor passes and every
  overrun yields the declared warning/degradation/refusal.

**F5b. Roster/profile budget enforcement**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · OPERATIONS**
- Gate: full roster × world × execution-profile matrix stays within F5a budgets; overruns are actionable, metered,
  and included in canary/release evidence.

### G. Release Safety Plane (cross-cutting and blocking)

**G0. Convergence and package no-regression**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · PERCEPTUAL**
- Re-run the frozen 40-slide reference with the primary decypher/comparator before shared core/serializer work.
  Replace the stale 2026-05-22 ledger with exact artifacts. Release green means: no repair, no real comparator
  differences, no unexplained XML/package differences, and no visible fidelity difference. Never mutate reference
  PPTX or post-process a generated PPTX as the fix.

**G1. Public black-box and saved-package privacy**  
Gates: **PRIVACY · PACKAGE**
- Extend scanners to decode BetterPPTX-owned sidecars and recurse structured payloads; test schemas, descriptions,
  recipes, results, errors, health, downloads, package metadata, and opaque handles. Remove current public
  TRELLIS/UniRig/Modal/provider fields and the provider-bearing handle scheme. Either remove private stock/source
  provenance from `ppt/betterpptx-edition.xml` or replace it with the minimum user-facing license data that is safe
  after decoding; retain private provenance server-side.
- Gate: fixed leak fixtures are detected; adversarial cold-client interrogation and decoded-package scans return
  only generic capabilities/user-facing license data, never source/process/repo/path/dependency/provider/host data.

**G2a. Reproducible immutable private-source build**  
Gates: **DETERMINISTIC · OPERATIONS**
- Require clean reviewed private SHA, dependency lock, tracked assets, source tree hash, image digest/SBOM, build
  logs, source-to-built smoke tests, and signed evidence ledger. The resolved build contract (owner-decided
  2026-07-14, clarified): **committed-dist** — `dist` is built locally on the dev host, committed, and the
  cloud image mirrors it exactly (`BUILD_DIST=0`, no in-cloud rebuild); reproducibility is proven by comparing
  the image's `dist` hashes against the committed `dist` at the pinned SHA. Worker artifacts/revisions are
  governed by the same provenance rule.
- Gate: another maintainer can reproduce the image from the recorded private revision and match declared source,
  dependency, asset, `dist`, worker, and image hashes without using the dirty local tree or public `origin`.

**G2b. Private canary routing and rollback**  
Gates: **PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- Execute through F1 only after its named preconditions. Route no public traffic; retain the prior image/worker
  revisions; run health, quality, cold-client, concurrent-instance, billing, and failure probes; then roll back.
- Gate: canary revision/digest and worker revisions match G2a evidence, all F1 probes pass, and rollback is timed,
  successful, and revalidated. Public promotion remains separate.

**G3. Durable, tenant-safe generated assets**  
Gates: **PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- Replace process-local authority with durable object storage + metadata, tenant authorization, opaque IDs,
  idempotent jobs, checksum validation, retention/deletion, and unavailable/expired recovery. Persist linked actor,
  poster, clip, calibration, timeline, license, and provenance manifests.
- Gate: create on instance A, read/embed on B, restart both, retry the job, deny another tenant, expire/delete, and
  recover from partial writes without data leak, double charge, or corrupt package.

**G4. Owner-controlled secrets and billing controls**  
Gates: **PRIVACY · OPERATIONS**
- Inventory at least `BETTERPPTX_MODEL3D_GEN_TOKEN`, `MODEL3D_ENDPOINT_TOKEN`,
  `BETTERPPTX_MODAL_FLUX_TOKEN`, `FLUX_ENDPOINT_TOKEN`, and `HF_TOKEN`, plus signing/webhook/payment/license keys.
  Keep raw values in Secret Manager. Private Mission Control shows redacted status, fingerprint, source, active
  version, last rotation, and dependents, with audited owner-only reveal/update/rotate/test actions.
- Gate: rotation and dependency tests pass without logging/telemetry/Firestore leakage; public paths cannot reveal
  values; break-glass and rollback are documented and audited.

**G5. Hash-keyed licensing/provenance authority**  
Gates: **LICENSING · PRIVACY**
- For every shipped model, clip, texture, poster, font, audio, and generated asset record source URL/owner,
  acquisition date, exact hash/version, license text/URL, attribution, allowed distribution mode, transformations,
  and approval. Adobe's [Mixamo FAQ](https://helpx.adobe.com/creative-cloud/faq/mixamo-faq.html) allows characters
  and animations in commercial projects, but that FAQ alone does not authorize reselling a reusable source clip
  library; require owner/legal approval for the exact bundled/distributed form.
- Gate: the build fails closed on missing/mismatched evidence; package attribution is correct and privacy-safe.

**G6. Supported-profile round-trip matrix**  
Gates: **PACKAGE · METROLOGY · PERCEPTUAL · OPERATIONS**
- Productize A4b as a release matrix with exact app/browser/OS versions, full/reduced/static policy, live playback,
  export, edit/save/reopen, package diff, fallback, and accessibility results.
- Gate: every advertised profile is green for its stated capability; unknown versions degrade honestly.

---

## 8. PHASES

| Phase | Contents | Exit gate |
|---|---|---|
| **P0 Reality, parity, and provenance** | freeze/track the evidence kernel and required inputs · G0 fresh three-oracle baseline/queue · G1 leak removal/scanner · G2a reproducible build contract · G4 secret inventory/control · G5 license authority | fresh convergence evidence and mismatch queue recorded; decoded-sidecar/public leak fixtures green; clean private SHA can build; owner secrets and all currently used assets have authoritative records |
| **P1 Compiler foundation** | A2a Timeline IR/order · A1 oracle/manifests · A3 runner · G3 durable asset plane · A4a platform-risk probe · F5a budgets/spend preflight | M1–M4 regenerate from tracked inputs; meaning-only M4 needs no manual bbox/workaround; deterministic/package/metrology suite green; restart/cross-instance/tenant tests green; unmeasured profiles fall back safely; pilot budgets approved |
| **P2 Performer** | B1 library v2 · B2 roster ≥4 (≥2 female) · B3 gaze · B4 stride · F2 onboarding | frozen ≤18 semantic library and roster manifests pass their declared target envelope, quality, license, size, and oracle gates |
| **P3 Direction and sensory language** | C1 arcs · C2 proposals · C3 layout co-design · D1/D2/D5 · E1/E2 | meaning-only fixtures produce stable storyboards, bounded layout/interaction plans, useful reduced/static variants, and perceptually approved direction |
| **P4 Private canary and polish** | C4/C5 · D3/D4 · G0-green A2b · E3 · F3 · G2b/F1 private canary · A4b/G6 · F5b · F4 | cold entitled private client passes durability, privacy, billing, rollback, profile, budget, flagship, and all regression gates; no public traffic |
| **P5 Public promotion** | final G0, G1, G2a/G2b, G3–G6 evidence refresh · owner release review · gradual traffic with monitoring/rollback | every advertised capability/profile and all seven gate classes are green on the exact image digest; owner explicitly authorizes promotion; rollback remains live |

The critical path is `P0 → P1 → P2 → P3 → P4 → P5`. Visual polish may prototype in parallel, but it cannot
waive an earlier gate. In particular, do not deploy the dirty local vertical slice to “see if it works”: first make
the build reproducible and private, remove product-surface/package leaks, and make generated assets durable.
P0's fresh baseline is sufficient to begin **track-local** P1 work; it does not make G0 green. Full G0 parity is
mandatory before A2b touches shared core/`dist`, before F1/G2b, and before promotion. Track work must not displace
the active comparator-first convergence queue.

P4 order is binding: finish local C4/C5/D3 and get G0 green → run A2b + full regression → finish E3/F3 and confirm
F5a/G1/G2a/G3/G4/G5 → run F1/G2b → collect A4b/G6 and F5b live evidence → approve F4 as the release fixture.

**Phase position at the 2026-08-01 refresh:** P0 provenance/convergence revalidation is open; P1 compiler and
durable-asset work is partially implemented but not suite-green; P2 has two locally proven fallback identities but
the 18-clip authoring library and ≥4-person roster gates are open; P3 direction primitives are locally green while
perceptual and corpus gates remain; P4/P5 are not entered. This ordering is deliberate: later avatar/image demos
do not waive the current G0, compiler, package, privacy, billing, licensing, or owner gates.

## 9. RISKS

1. **Convergence displacement** — Synergy work can distract from the workspace's parity mission or regress shared
   serialization. Owner: G0 + frozen references + three-oracle checks before/after every shared-core change.
2. **Local-evidence illusion** — untracked files and a warm process make M4 look deployable. Owner: P0 tracked
   evidence kernel, immutable private SHA/image, A3, G2a/G2b, and G3 restart/cross-instance tests.
3. **Oracle/platform drift** — all current laws came from one incompletely recorded Windows/CreateVideo profile.
   Owner: exact v2 manifests, A1/A3, A4a/A4b/G6; degrade unknown profiles instead of guessing.
4. **Compiler-order debt** — pre-layout solving, fixed widescreen constants, duplicate beats, unused tolerance, and
   non-semantic clip selection can create locally plausible but globally wrong plans. Owner: A2a/C3 + seeded
   property corpus and one-replan contract.
5. **Black-box leakage** — current help/results/handles and decoded edition sidecar reveal implementation details.
   Owner: G1 fail-closed scanners and product-generic public contracts before any canary/public artifact.
6. **Asset loss or cross-tenant access** — process-local cache and incomplete manifests cannot survive a real
   service topology. Owner: G3 durable, authorized, idempotent lifecycle with failure injection.
7. **License/supply-chain mismatch** — commercial project permission is not automatically permission to bundle or
   resell reusable source assets. Owner: G5 exact-form legal approval, hashes, attribution, SBOM, fail-closed build.
8. **Billing/secret launch risk** — new generation paths can incur cost, double-charge retries, or strand an owner
   behind inaccessible credentials. Owner: F3/G4 reconciliation, owner controls, rotation, audit, rollback.
9. **File/performance explosion** — roster × clips × audio/posters can make decks slow or uneconomic. Owner: F5a/F5b
   profile budgets and explicit degradation/refusal thresholds.
10. **Motion/accessibility failure** — a living presenter can obscure content, induce unwanted motion, or become
    the only information carrier. Owner: D5, accessibility rubric, actor-hidden test, and full/reduced/static policy.
11. **Scope gravity** — each layer invites infinite polish. Owner: phase exits and evidence classes; a local demo,
    assertion count, or owner impression is useful evidence only for the gate it actually satisfies.

## 10. REQUIRED RELEASE INVARIANTS (enforced by the named gates)

These are the release contract, not a claim about the current working tree. Any item identified as blocked/red in
§0, §2, or §6 remains unsatisfied until its named gate supplies evidence.

- Reference PPTX files are immutable; regenerated packages are never post-processed as a product fix. Repair-free
  open, comparator, direct XML/package inspection, and visible fidelity are all required for parity.
- Repair/package integrity outranks convergence and cosmetic work. Do not use regex for XML manipulation in
  `src/`; fix extraction, codegen, object-model serialization, package assembly, or comparator coverage with the
  narrowest safe preservation required.
- Public surfaces are a product black box: generic capabilities, opaque authorized handles, and safe user-facing
  license data only. Encoding/compression never creates privacy.
- Production comes from a clean reviewed private commit and immutable image digest. All required source, assets,
  manifests, licenses, and built artifacts are hash-linked; rollback is tested.
- Billing/payment/license/webhook/launch secrets remain in secret storage and are owner-visible/customizable only
  through audited private controls; public surfaces and ordinary logs never reveal them.
- Generated assets are durable, tenant-authorized, idempotent, lifecycle-managed, and inseparable from versioned
  oracle, semantic, provenance, and licensing manifests.
- Compilation follows §2.6: actual slide dimensions, narration-led Timeline IR, preliminary layout before solving,
  semantic clip filtering, at most one deterministic replan, then an immutable final plan/storyboard. Never re-solve
  the expanded spec.
- ≤18 clips per model; one 3D-scene clip per model instance; target-aware gaze must fit the versioned slot contract.
- Within the currently measured profile: square 3D windows/posters; viewport `h×1454545/margin`; calibrated
  h∈[3.5,6.5], m∈[1,2]; full-clip durations and plateau contacts; no claimed mid-clip freeze.
- No zero-length bufferViews; NORMALs on every rendered primitive; extension-aware texture scans; owner-final 512
  texture caps and matte materials; poster/static output remains a designed information-complete fallback.
- Native animation objects use `duration`/`delay` (never `*Ms`), custom paths carry validated `pathData`, and shape
  styling uses `style.fill`. The current motionPathData-only choreography helper is a known red gate. Verify timing
  in final XML when click-group rebucketing matters.
- Filmstrips/human rubric prove 3D motion quality; numeric COM/live-render measurements prove behavior; deterministic
  tests prove compiler logic; package/privacy/operations/licensing gates remain independent.
- `PYTHONUTF8=1`; the local presenter-director composition self-test stays green (currently 24/24) but never
  substitutes for compiler/solver, A3, or G gates.

**2026-08-02 engine-authoring continuation:** semantic image framing is now implemented in the shared compiler/library

semantic contract; the full development aggregate is **GREEN: 86 registered, 83 PASS, 3 optional live skips, 0
